# 第1章 自然地理
magma | n. 岩浆 | Some types of minerals are made when ___ cools and solidifies.
erupt | v. 爆发，喷发；（斑疹等）突然出现 | Hot lava ___ from the crust.
volcano | n. 火山 | The ___ erupted and two small towns were buried under metres of hot ash.
crust | n. 地壳；外壳 | The ___ on the snow was thick enough for us to walk on it.

core | n. 中心，核心；地核 ／ n. 果核 | The earth's inner ___ is thought to be made largely of solid iron.
atmosphere | n. 大气层，大气圈；气氛 | Almost all weather occurs in the lowest layer of the earth's ___.
hydrosphere | n. 水圈；大气中的水汽 | All the water of the earth's surface is included in the ___.
lithosphere | n. 岩石圈 | The hydrosphere and the ___ together form the earth's surface.
oxygen | n. 氧气 | Hydrogen and ___ are gases.
oxide | n. 氧化物 | Rust forms when iron reacts with oxygen to produce iron ___.
carbon dioxide | n. 二氧化碳 | Soft drinks usually contain ___.
hydrogen | n. 氢气 | Two parts ___, one part oxygen, you'll get water every time.
longitude | n. 经度 | The captain determines the latitude and ___ of his ship.
latitude | n. 纬度 | Our position is 40 degrees north ___.
horizon | n. 地平线；[~s]眼界，见识 | I could see a ship on the ___.
altitude | n. 高度，海拔 | We are currently flying at an ___ of 8,000 metres.
disaster | n. 灾难 | The earthquake was the worst natural ___ the country had seen in decades.

jeopardise | v. 危害，危及（= jeopardize） | If you are rude to the boss, your chances of success may be ___.
destructive | adj. 破坏性的，有害的 | Floods are the most ___ natural hazard in this low-lying region.

# 第12章 饮食健康

# 第22章 时间日期
daily | adj. 每日的 n. 日报 | The best treatment for the pain is ___ exercises.
monthly | adj. 每月的 adv. 每月一次 n. 月刊 | She pays the rent in ___ instalments.
quarterly | adj. 季度的 adv. 按季度地 n. 季刊 | This journal is bimonthly, and that one ___.
annual | adj. 每年的;一年的 n. 年报 | The firm has an ___ turnover of 800 million pounds.
yearly | adj. 每年的;一年的 adv. 每年地 | The ___ conference will be held next month.
anniversary | n. 周年纪念日 | Jack and Kim celebrated their 20th wedding ___ in January.
era | n. 时代,年代,纪元 | We are living in the information ___.
present | adj. 现在的 n. 目前;礼物,赠送物 | There's no certain cure for this illness at ___.
contemporary | adj. 当代的;同时代的 | These clothes are more ___ and wearable.
medieval | adj. 中世纪的;守旧的 | The town still has its ___ walls and castle.
century | n. 世纪;百年 | It was considered one of the best films of the 20th ___.
millennium | n. 一千年,千年期;千禧年 | What did you do to celebrate the new ___?
million | num. 百万;大量 | It was estimated that the damage was over one ___ dollars.
billion | num. 十亿;大量 | The human brain contains ___ of nerve cells.
dozen | n. 一打,十二个;十来个 | The turkey is large enough to feed a ___.
dawn | n. 黎明;开端 v. 破晓 | We set sail at ___ and headed for New York.
midday | n. 正午,中午 | We finished work at ___.
midnight | n. 午夜,子夜,半夜 | According to astronomers, the best time for viewing is between ___ and dawn.
overnight | adv. 在晚上;突然,一夜之间 | I stayed ___ at a friend's home.
night | n. 夜,夜间;晚上;夜场 | Owls hunt at ___ and sleep during the day.
modern | adj. 现代的;近代的 | The girl can't adjust herself to the whirl of ___ life in this big city.
recent | adj. 新近的,最近的 | The vase looked old but was a ___ fake.
up-to-date | adj. 直到目前的;现代的 | ___ information about the research on climate change can be found on this website.
first | ord. 第一 adv. 最初,第一 | What is your ___ impression of Beijing?
secondly | adv. 第二,其次 | To start with, we don't have enough money. ___, we're too busy.
finally | adv. 最后,终于;决定性地 | After three days of searching they ___ found the missing dog.
finish | v. 完成,结束 n. 结尾,最后部分 | When would you ___ your college course?
immediately | adv. 立即;直接地 | I will then ___ send confirmation and the application process begins.
instant | n. 瞬间 adj. 立即的 | Just for an ___ I thought she was going to refuse.
moment | n. 片刻;时刻 | Love is a ___ that lasts forever.
minute | n. 分,分钟;一会儿 adj. 细微的 | It's only several ___ from here to the supermarket.
prior | adj. 在先的,在前的 | I have a ___ appointment, so I can't go with you.
former | adj. 以前的;前者的 pron. 前者 | Many of his ___ supporters voted against him.
preceding | adj. 在前的,在先的 | She had been in Mexico the ___ summer.
precedent | n. 先例;常规 | They were determined to break with ___.
foremost | adj. 最前的;最重要的 | He is one of the ___ scientists in China.
opening | adj. 开始的 n. 开始,开端 | The book's ___ is dull, but the last chapters are interesting.
initially | adv. 最初 | ___, I disagreed with their proposal, but later I changed my mind.
intermediate | adj. 中间的 | This country is still at an ___ stage of development.
midst | n. 中部,中间 | We're lucky to have such a man in our ___.
meantime | n. [in the ~]其间,同时;[for the ~]眼下,暂时 | The train leaves at six; in the ___, let's have a coffee.
meanwhile | adv. 同时,其间 | The media, ___, has blown hot and cold on the affair.
simultaneous | adj. 同时发生(或进行)的 | This event was almost ___ with that one.
ongoing | adj. 进行中的;继续存在的 | The discussions are still ___.
everlasting | adj. 永恒的 | The man told the woman that he loved her with an ___ love.
permanent | adj. 永久的 | We have no ___ friends or enemies, only interests.
punctual | adj. 准时的,守时的 | He is always reliable and ___.
duration | n. 持续时间;期间 | Difficulties will be experienced throughout the ___ of the war.
consecutive | adj. 连续不断的 | He has been absent from school for four ___ days.
periodically | adv. 周期性地,定期地 | Sea mammals must emerge ___ to breathe.
imminent | adj. (尤指不愉快的事)即将发生的,逼近的 | They are unaware of the ___ danger.
incidentally | adv. 偶然地;顺便提一句 | ___, here's good news for you.
